Judges 9 · grammar, 5–30%

11 from-scratch questions × 7 conditions × 7 reader settings = 539 responses. Conditions are clean and six independently selected grammar doses. All defect versions passed the current grammar and meaning checks.

Dose counts affected clean Chinese source characters, with ±2 percentage-point tolerance. The generation bank uses a soft preference of 30% misselection and 70% misordering by error instances. Accuracy is against the clean-source key. E (“无法判断”), wrong A–D answers, and invalid responses are reported separately. One answer changes a score by 9.1 percentage points; this small run is exploratory.

Same seven model settings and reader-specific prompts as prior dose experiments; temperature 0, 8192-token context, 2500 generated-token cap including thinking. Fresh conversation per question and condition; raw responses and thinking retained. Clean baseline included. Exact request checkpoints reused only when resuming this run. Unmatched nonempty answers from the first four settings use the established GPT-4.1-mini choice mapper, receiving only response text and choices. No passages were published.
